The system integrates four components: (1) a Query Tree Classifier computing a Query Complexity Index from weighted linguistic signals to route queries to either a rapid single-step path or an adaptive-depth hierarchical path; (2) a Tree-Based Retrieval module that recursively decomposes complex queries into focused sub-queries calibrated to predicted complexity; (3) an Adaptive Pruning Module employing two-stage filtering combining quantitative similarity gating with semantic relevance evaluation to suppress exponential node growth; and (4) a Retrieval Reranking Layer featuring a deduplicator-first pipeline and global LLM rescoring for production efficiency. Evaluation on the NLU++ benchmark (2,693 multi-intent queries across Banking and Hotel domains) yields 29.07% Subset Accuracy and 71.79% Micro-F1, a 9.7% relative improvement over fixed-depth baselines, while reducing latency by 37.6%, LLM invocations by 43.0%, and token consumption by 9.8%. Depth-wise analysis reveals that 26.92% of queries resolve within three seconds (2.45s mean latency) via single-step routing (d=0: 37.9% Subset Accuracy, 74.8% Micro-F1), while token consumption scales by 4.9x across depths, validating complexity-aware resource allocation and establishing Pareto-optimal balance across accuracy, latency, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [147] [Aligning with Your Own Voice: Self-Corrected Preference Learning for Hallucination Mitigation in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24395)
*Byeonggeuk Lim,JungMin Yun,Junehyoung Kwon,Kyeonghyun Kim,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AVES-DPO框架，通过模型自有的内在知识生成分布内数据，基于共识验证机制诊断幻觉并引导自矫正，仅用5.2k样本即可有效缓解LVLMs的幻觉问题，优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好学习的LVLM幻觉缓解方法依赖专有模型构建偏好数据集，导致专有模型与目标模型之间存在分布不匹配，阻碍了高效的对齐。

Method: 提出AVES-DPO框架，利用模型内在知识生成分布内数据；采用基于共识的验证机制诊断多种幻觉类型；引导模型进行自矫正，生成与模型内部分布严格兼容的偏好对。

Result: 在幻觉缓解任务上超越现有基线方法，且仅需5.2k训练样本。

Conclusion: 通过利用模型自身知识进行自矫正偏好对齐，可以有效解决分布不匹配问题，高效缓解LVLMs的幻觉。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) frequently suffer from hallucinations. Existing preference learning-based approaches largely rely on proprietary models to construct preference datasets. We identify that this reliance introduces a distributional mismatch between the proprietary and target models that hinders efficient alignment. To address this, we propose Alignment via VErified Self-correction DPO (AVES-DPO), a framework that aligns LVLMs using in-distribution data derived from the model's intrinsic knowledge. Our approach employs a consensus-based verification mechanism to diagnose diverse hallucinations and guides the model to self-correct, thereby generating preference pairs strictly compatible with its internal distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AVES-DPO surpasses existing baselines in hallucination mitigation while requiring only 5.2k samples.

</details>


### [148] [PhysNote: Self-Knowledge Notes for Evolvable Physical Reasoning in Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24443)
*Sinin Zhang,Yunfei Xie,Yuxuan Cheng,Haoyu Zhang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysNote框架，通过自生成"知识笔记"外化物理知识，解决VLM在动态物理场景中的时空身份漂移和推理洞察易变性问题，在PhysBench上取得56.68%准确率，比最佳多智能体基线提升4.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在教材式物理问题上表现良好，但在需要时间一致性和跨帧因果推理的动态真实场景中频繁失败，主要面临两个根本挑战：(1) 时空身份漂移——物体在连续帧中丢失物理身份，破坏因果链；(2) 推理洞察易变性——模型偶尔产生正确推理但无法巩固复用。

Method: PhysNote是一个智能体框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 时空规范化——稳定动态感知；(2) 层次化知识仓库——将自生成洞察组织成层级结构；(3) 迭代推理循环——在视觉证据中验证假设，再将验证后的知识巩固存储。

Result: 在PhysBench上，PhysNote达到56.68%整体准确率，比最佳多智能体基线提升4.96%，并在全部四个物理推理领域均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 通过外部化知识笔记和迭代验证机制，PhysNote有效解决了时空身份漂移和推理洞察易变性问题，证明了显式知识管理对提升VLM动态物理推理能力的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on textbook-style physics problems, yet they frequently fail when confronted with dynamic real-world scenarios that require temporal consistency and causal reasoning across frames. We identify two fundamental challenges underlying these failures: (1) spatio-temporal identity drift, where objects lose their physical identity across successive frames and break causal chains, and (2) volatility of inference-time insights, where a model may occasionally produce correct physical reasoning but never consolidates it for future reuse. To address these challenges, we propose PhysNote, an agentic framework that enables VLMs to externalize and refine physical knowledge through self-generated "Knowledge Notes." PhysNote stabilizes dynamic perception through spatio-temporal canonicalization, organizes self-generated insights into a hierarchical knowledge repository, and drives an iterative reasoning loop that grounds hypotheses in visual evidence before consolidating verified knowledge. Experiments on PhysBench demonstrate that PhysNote achieves 56.68% overall accuracy, a 4.96% improvement over the best multi-agent baseline, with consistent gains across all four physical reasoning domains.

</details>


### [149] [Agentic clinical reasoning over longitudinal myeloma records: a retrospective evaluation against expert consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24473)
*Johannes Moll,Jannik Lübberstedt,Christoph Nuernbergk,Jacob Stroh,Luisa Mertens,Anna Purcarea,Christopher Zirn,Zeineb Benchaaben,Fabian Drexel,Hartmut Häntze,Anirudh Narayanan,Friedrich Puttkammer,Andrei Zhukov,Jacqueline Lammert,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Markus Graf,Marion Högner,Marcus Makowski,Florian Bassermann,Lisa C. Adams,Jiazhen Pan,Daniel Rueckert,Krischan Braitsch,Keno K. Bressem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于LLM的智能体推理系统在多发性骨髓瘤纵向病历（811名患者，44,962份文档）中的表现。智能体系统达到79.6%的专家一致率，超越迭代RAG（75.4%）和全文本输入（75.8%），在复杂问题和长病历上优势更显著（+9.4 pp和+13.5 pp）。系统错误率（12.2%）与专家分歧率（13.6%）相当，但临床严重性错误比例更高（57.8% vs 18.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统能否在合成多发性骨髓瘤患者跨数十年的分散临床证据时达到接近专家一致的水平尚未被验证。

Method: 回顾性研究，纳入811名骨髓瘤患者（2001-2026年）的44,962份文档和1,334,677个实验室值，外部验证使用MIMIC-IV。比较智能体推理系统与单次RAG、迭代RAG和全文本输入在469个患者-问题对上的表现。参考标签由四位肿瘤科医生双重标注并由资深血液科医生裁决。

Result: 智能体系统达79.6%一致率，高于迭代RAG（75.4%）和全文本输入（75.8%）。复杂问题上优势+9.4 pp，长病历上+13.5 pp。系统错误率12.2%与专家分歧13.6%相近，但57.8%系统错误具临床意义，专家仅18.8%。

Conclusion: 智能体推理是唯一超越共享性能天花板的方法，优势集中在最复杂问题和最长病历。但残留系统错误的临床后果更严重，需在前瞻性常规诊疗中评估后才能转化为患者获益。

Abstract: Multiple myeloma is managed through sequential lines of therapy over years to decades, with each decision depending on cumulative disease history distributed across dozens to hundreds of heterogeneous clinical documents. Whether LLM-based systems can synthesise this evidence at a level approaching expert agreement has not been established. A retrospective evaluation was conducted on longitudinal clinical records of 811 myeloma patients treated at a tertiary centre (2001-2026), covering 44,962 documents and 1,334,677 laboratory values, with external validation on MIMIC-IV. An agentic reasoning system was compared against single-pass retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), iterative RAG, and full-context input on 469 patient-question pairs from 48 templates at three complexity levels. Reference labels came from double annotation by four oncologists with senior haematologist adjudication. Iterative RAG and full-context input converged on a shared ceiling (75.4% vs 75.8%, p = 1.00). The agentic system reached 79.6% concordance (95% CI 76.4-82.8), exceeding both baselines (+3.8 and +4.2 pp; p = 0.006 and 0.007). Gains rose with question complexity, reaching +9.4 pp on criteria-based synthesis (p = 0.032), and with record length, reaching +13.5 pp in the top decile (n = 10). The system error rate (12.2%) was comparable to expert disagreement (13.6%), but severity was inverted: 57.8% of system errors were clinically significant versus 18.8% of expert disagreements. Agentic reasoning was the only approach to exceed the shared ceiling, with gains concentrated on the most complex questions and longest records. The greater clinical consequence of residual system errors indicates that prospective evaluation in routine care is required before these findings translate into patient benefit.

</details>


### [150] [MIMIC: A Generative Multimodal Foundation Model for Biomolecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24506)
*Siavash Golkar,Jake Kovalic,Irina Espejo Morales,Samuel Sledzieski,Minhuan Li,Ksenia Sokolova,Geraud Krawezik,Alberto Bietti,Claudia Skok Gibbs,Roman Klypa,Shengwei Xiong,Francois Lanusse,Liam Parker,Kyunghyun Cho,Miles Cranmer,Tom Hehir,Michael McCabe,Lucas Meyer,Rudy Morel,Payel Mukhopadhyay,Mariel Pettee,Helen Qu,Jeff Shen,David Fouhey,Hadi Sotoudeh,Vikram Mulligan,Pilar Cossio,Sonya M. Hanson,Alisha N. Jones,Olga G. Troyanskaya,Shirley Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIMIC是一个多模态生成式基础模型，在核酸、蛋白质、进化、结构、调控和语义等多个生物模态上训练，能够通过任意模态子集进行条件生成、预测和约束设计，在RNA剪接预测、蛋白质设计和实验上下文建模等任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学功能源于序列、结构、调控、进化和细胞上下文等多重耦合约束，但现有基础模型通常局限于单一模态或固定前向任务，缺乏统一的多模态生成框架。

Method: 提出MIMIC模型，采用分轨编码器-解码器架构，在部分观测的生物分子状态下，以任意子集的观测模态为条件，重建或生成缺失的分子状态组件；同时构建了LORE多模态对齐数据集。

Result: 多模态条件显著提升序列重建质量；在RNA和蛋白质下游任务上达到最优性能，包括剪接预测；实现异构体感知推理；在RNA编辑设计中识别HBB突变校正方案；在蛋白质设计中生成高置信度结合序列；支持实验上下文依赖的RNA化学探测建模。

Conclusion: MIMIC的对齐多模态生成建模为统一表示学习、条件预测和约束生物分子设计提供了强大的基础框架。

Abstract: Biological function emerges from coupled constraints across sequence, structure, regulation, evolution, and cellular context, yet most foundation models in biology are trained within one modality or for a fixed forward task. We present MIMIC, a generative multimodal foundation model trained on our newly curated and aligned dataset, LORE, linking nucleic acid, protein, evolutionary, structural, regulatory, and semantic/contextual modalities within partially observed biomolecular states. MIMIC uses a split-track encoder-decoder architecture to condition on arbitrary subsets of observed modalities and reconstruct or generate missing components of molecular state across the genome, transcriptome, and proteome. Multimodal conditioning consistently improves MIMIC's sequence reconstruction relative to sequence-only inputs, while its learned representations enable state-of-the-art performance on RNA and protein downstream tasks. MIMIC achieves state-of-the-art splicing prediction, and its joint generative formulation enables isoform-aware inference that further improves performance. Beyond prediction, the same generative framework supports constrained design. For RNA, MIMIC identifies corrective edits in a clinically relevant HBB splice-disrupting mutation without reverting it by using evolutionary and structural signals. For proteins, jointly conditioning on shape and surface chemistry of PD-L1 and hACE2 binding sites produces diverse, high-confidence sequences with strong in silico support for target binding. Finally, MIMIC uses experimental context as semantic conditioning to model assay-dependent RNA chemical probing, rather than treating context as a fixed output. Together, these results position MIMIC's aligned multimodal generative modeling as a strong foundation for unifying representation learning, conditional prediction, and constrained biomolecular design within a single model.

</details>


### [151] [STELLAR-E: a Synthetic, Tailored, End-to-end LLM Application Rigorous Evaluator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24544)
*Alessio Sordo,Lingxiao Du,Meeka-Hanna Lenisa,Evgeny Bogdanov,Maxim Romanovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STELLAR-E，一个全自动合成数据集生成系统，通过两阶段流程（改进的TGRT Self-Instruct框架+评估管道）生成高质量、可控规模的多领域合成数据集，在LLM评估中达到与现有语言基准相当的质量（+5.7%），为缺少领域特定和语言特定基准的场景提供可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同领域的部署需要领域特定和语言特定的评估数据集，但收集此类数据集面临隐私问题、监管限制和人工创建耗时等挑战。现有的自动化基准测试方法受限于依赖预存在数据、可扩展性差、单领域聚焦和缺乏多语言支持。

Method: 两阶段系统：(1) 修改TGRT Self-Instruct框架构建合成数据引擎，实现可控的自定义合成数据集生成，最小化人工输入且不依赖现有数据集；(2) 评估管道整合统计指标和LLM-based指标，评估合成数据集在LLM应用评估中的适用性。

Result: 合成数据集在LLM-as-a-judge评分中平均比现有语言特定基准高出5.7%，证明其质量与现有基准相当，可用于全面评估大小型LLM。但真实数据集对LLM（尤其是小型模型）仍略具挑战性。

Conclusion: 建立了可扩展且领域自适应的基准测试框架，支持LLM应用的公平评估，提供比人工方法更快的替代方案，实现高效自动化的质量保证循环。

Abstract: The increasing reliance on Large Language Models (LLMs) across diverse sectors highlights the need for robust domain-specific and language-specific evaluation datasets; however, the collection of such datasets is challenging due to privacy concerns, regulatory restrictions, and the time cost for manual creation. Existing automated benchmarking methods are often limited by relying on pre-existing data, poor scalability, single-domain focus, and lack of multilingual support. We present STELLAR-E - a fully automated system to generate high-quality synthetic datasets of custom size, using minimal human inputs without depending on existing datasets. The system is structured in two stages: (1) We modify the TGRT Self-Instruct framework to create a synthetic data engine that enables controllable, custom synthetic dataset generation, and (2) an evaluation pipeline incorporating statistical and LLM-based metrics to assess the applicability of the synthetic dataset for LLM-based application evaluations. The synthetic datasets reach an average difference of +5.7% in terms of LLM-as-a-judge scores against existing language-specific benchmarks, demonstrating comparable quality for comprehensive assessment of big and small LLMs. While real datasets remain slightly more challenging for LLMs especially for smaller models, this work establishes a scalable and domain-adaptable benchmarking framework that supports fair evaluation of LLM applications, offering a faster alternative to manual approaches and enabling high-efficiency automated quality assurance cycles.

</details>


### [152] [Hierarchical Behaviour Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24558)
*Michael Tryfan Matthews,Anssi Kanervisto,Jakob Foerster,Pierluca D'Oro,Scott Fujimoto,Mikael Henaff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层行为空间（HBS）方法，通过让控制器为每个选项指定奖励函数的线性组合，而非使用单一奖励函数，从而诱导更丰富的行为空间。在NetHack学习环境上的实验表明该方法表现优异，且分层带来的收益主要源于增强的探索而非长期推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层强化学习通常为每个选项预定义单一奖励函数，限制了行为的表达力。作者希望利用奖励函数的线性组合，让控制器能够灵活地组合多种奖励信号，从而诱导出更丰富的行为空间。

Method: 提出分层行为空间（HBS），核心思想是让高层控制器为每个选项指定多个奖励函数的线性组合（权重），而非使用固定的单一奖励函数。这样低层策略可以在由这些组合奖励函数所诱导的行为空间中学习，从而表达更多样化的策略。

Result: 在NetHack学习环境上进行评估，HBS展现出强大的性能。通过一系列实验分析发现，层次结构带来的性能提升主要来源于增强的探索能力，而非传统认为的长时序推理能力。

Conclusion: HBS通过奖励函数线性组合的方式有效地扩展了分层强化学习中选项的表达能力。重要的是，实验揭示了一个反直觉的发现：分层强化学习在本方法中的成功关键并非长时序推理，而是促进了更有效的探索。

Abstract: Recent work in hierarchical reinforcement learning has shown success in scaling to billions of timesteps when learning over a set of predefined option reward functions. We show that, instead of using a single reward function per option, the reward functions can be effectively used to induce a space of behaviours, by letting the controller specify linear combinations over reward functions, allowing a more expressive set of policies to be represented. We call this method Hierarchical Behaviour Spaces (HBS). We evaluate HBS on the NetHack Learning Environment, demonstrating strong performance. We conduct a series of experiments and determine that, perhaps going against conventional wisdom, the benefits of hierarchy in our method come from increased exploration rather than long term reasoning.

</details>


### [153] [Towards Lawful Autonomous Driving: Deriving Scenario-Aware Driving Requirements from Traffic Laws and Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24562)
*Bowen Jian,Rongjie Yu,Hong Wang,Liqiang Wang,Zihang Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于交通场景分类法和大语言模型的自动驾驶法规合规性推理管线，通过节点锚定编码层次化语义，在中文交通法规和OnSite数据集上显著提升了法规-场景匹配准确率，并构建了实车合规监控系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动驾驶法规合规方法使用形式化逻辑语言显式指定行为约束，但人工工作量大、扩展性差且维护成本高；直接使用大语言模型推导法规要求又因缺乏结构化场景的显式基础与推理而导致检索不精确。

Method: 提出一种新颖管线，通过节点锚点（node-wise anchors）编码层次化语义，将大语言模型的推理过程锚定在交通场景分类法（traffic scenario taxonomy）中，实现结构化场景下的法规-场景匹配。

Result: 在中文交通法规和OnSite数据集（5,897个场景）上，法规-场景匹配准确率提升29.1%，强制性要求推导准确率提升36.9%，禁止性要求推导准确率提升38.2%。

Conclusion: 通过构建自动驾驶法规合规层和实车实时合规监控器，验证了该方法在真实世界中的适用性，为自动驾驶的开发、部署和监管提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Driving in compliance with traffic laws and regulations is a basic requirement for human drivers, yet autonomous vehicles (AVs) can violate these requirements in diverse real-world scenarios. To encode law compliance into AV systems, conventional approaches use formal logic languages to explicitly specify behavioral constraints, but this process is labor-intensive, hard to scale, and costly to maintain. With recent advances in artificial intelligence, it is promising to leverage large language models (LLMs) to derive legal requirements from traffic laws and regulations. However, without explicitly grounding and reasoning in structured traffic scenarios, LLMs often retrieve irrelevant provisions or miss applicable ones, yielding imprecise requirements. To address this, we propose a novel pipeline that grounds LLM reasoning in a traffic scenario taxonomy through node-wise anchors that encode hierarchical semantics. On Chinese traffic laws and OnSite dataset (5,897 scenarios), our method improves law-scenario matching by 29.1\% and increases the accuracy of derived mandatory and prohibitive requirements by 36.9\% and 38.2\%, respectively. We further demonstrate real-world applicability by constructing a law-compliance layer for AV navigation and developing an onboard, real-time compliance monitor for in-field testing, providing a solid foundation for future AV development, deployment, and regulatory oversight.

</details>


### [154] [FastOMOP: A Foundational Architecture for Reliable Agentic Real-World Evidence Generation on OMOP CDM data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24572)
*Niko Moeller-Grell,Shihao Shenzhang,Zhangshu Joshua Jiang,Richard JB Dobson,Vishnu V Chandrabalan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FastOMOP 是一个开源多智能体架构，通过将治理、可观测性和编排三层基础设施与可插拔智能体团队分离，解决了基于 OMOP CDM 的真实世界证据自动化生成中的安全性、灵活性和可审计性问题。该架构在流程边界强制执行确定性验证，确保即使智能体被攻破或产生幻觉也无法绕过安全控制。在三个数据集上的验证显示可靠性达 0.84-0.94，对抗性和超出范围阻断率完美，证明可靠性差距源于架构而非模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OMOP CDM 数据库虽有近十亿患者数据，但从中生成真实世界证据仍需人工操作；LLM 和多智能体系统虽有潜力，但智能体系统存在涌现行为、协调失败和安全风险，缺乏能确保灵活、安全且可审计的 RWE 自动化基础设施。

Method: 提出 FastOMOP 三层分离架构：治理层在流程边界通过确定性验证独立于智能体推理强制执行安全控制；可观测性层和编排层与可插拔智能体团队解耦；智能体团队（表型分析、研究设计、统计分析）通过受控工具暴露继承安全保证。使用自然语言到 SQL 智能体团队在 Synthea、MIMIC-IV 和真实 NHS 数据集上验证。

Result: FastOMOP 在三个 OMOP CDM 数据集上可靠性评分达 0.84-0.94，对抗性和超出范围阻断率均为完美（100%），证明流程边界治理独立于模型选择提供安全保证。

Conclusion: 真实世界证据部署中的可靠性差距本质上是架构问题而非模型能力问题，FastOMOP 建立了渐进式 RWE 自动化的受控架构。

Abstract: The Observational Medical Outcomes Partnership Common Data Model (OMOP CDM), maintained by the Observational Health Data Sciences and Informatics (OHDSI) collaboration, enabled the harmonisation of electronic health records data of nearly one billion patients in 83 countries. Yet generating real-world evidence (RWE) from these repositories remains a manual process requiring clinical, epidemiological and technical expertise. LLMs and multi-agent systems have shown promise for clinical tasks, but RWE automation exposes a fundamental challenge: agentic systems introduce emergent behaviours, coordination failures and safety risks that existing approaches fail to govern. No infrastructure exists to ensure agentic RWE generation is flexible, safe and auditable across the lifecycle. We introduce FastOMOP, an open-source multi-agent architecture that addresses this gap by separating three infrastructure layers, governance, observability and orchestration, from pluggable agent-teams. Governance is enforced at the process boundary through deterministic validation independent of agent reasoning, ensuring no compromised or hallucinating agent can bypass safety controls. Agent teams for phenotyping, study design and statistical analysis inherit these guarantees through controlled tool exposure. We validated FastOMOP using a natural-language-to-SQL agent team across three OMOP CDM datasets: synthetic data from Synthea, MIMIC-IV and a real-world NHS dataset from Lancashire Teaching Hospitals (IDRIL). FastOMOP achieved reliability scores of 0.84-0.94 with perfect adversarial and out-of-scope block rates, demonstrating process-boundary governance delivers safety guarantees independent of model choice. These results indicate that the reliability gap in RWE deployment is architectural rather than model capability, and establish FastOMOP as a governed architecture for progressive RWE automation.

</details>


### [155] [A systematic evaluation of vision-language models for observational astronomical reasoning tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24589)
*Wenke Ren,Hengxiao Guo,Wenwen Zuo,Xiaoman Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AstroVLBench基准，系统评估了6个前沿VLM在天文学五大模态任务上的表现，发现模型性能高度依赖模态、显著弱于专业方法，且物理知识引导比现象学提示更有效，数值表格比渲染图表现更好，准确率单独不足以衡量科学可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM被越来越多地用作科学数据解释的通用工具，但其在真实、多模态天文观测数据上的可靠性尚未得到系统测试。

Method: 构建AstroVLBench基准，包含4100+专家验证实例，覆盖光学成像、射电干涉、多波段测光、时域光变曲线和光学光谱五项任务；评估6个前沿VLM；进行机制消融实验，比较现象学提示与物理提示的效果，以及数值表格与渲染图的对比。

Result: 模型性能高度依赖模态，Gemini 3 Pro整体最稳定但各模型各有强弱；所有模型显著弱于领域专业方法；物理提示优于现象学提示，能减少类别偏差；数值表格比渲染图提升最多13个百分点；模型可能基于现象学线索得到正确预测但给出物理不精确的理由。

Conclusion: 该工作为VLM在天文学观测中的系统性多模态基线提供了首个基准，指出了当前模型在表示、基础推理和推理方面的关键瓶颈，表明仅凭准确率不足以判断模型在科学场景中的可信度。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly proposed as general-purpose tools for scientific data interpretation, yet their reliability on real astronomical observations across diverse modalities remains untested. We present AstroVLBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising over 4,100 expert-verified instances across five tasks spanning optical imaging, radio interferometry, multi-wavelength photometry, time-domain light curves, and optical spectroscopy. Evaluating six frontier models, we find that performance is strongly modality-dependent: while one model (Gemini 3 Pro) emerges as the most consistently capable across tasks, task-specific strengths vary, and all models substantially underperform domain-specialized methods. Mechanistic ablations reveal that performance depends not only on directing attention to salient visual features but also on grounding those features in physical knowledge. Phenomenological prompts describing what to look for improve accuracy by sharpening model focus, but physical prompts explaining why those features matter perform better overall and yield more balanced classifications with reduced class-specific bias. Consistent with this picture, presenting the underlying one-dimensional measurements directly as numerical tables instead of rendered plots yields up to 13 percentage points improvement. Reasoning quality analysis further demonstrates that, without explicit physical grounding, models may reach correct predictions from phenomenologically plausible cues while providing physically imprecise justifications, establishing that accuracy alone is insufficient for trustworthy scientific deployment. These findings provide the first systematic, multi-modal baselines for VLMs in observational astronomy and identify the specific representation, grounding, and reasoning bottlenecks where current models fail.

</details>


### [156] [XGRAG: A Graph-Native Framework for Explaining KG-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24623)
*Zhuoling Li,Ha Linh Hong Tran Nguyen,Valeria Bladinieres,Maxim Romanovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XGRAG框架，通过图基扰动策略量化知识图谱组件对GraphRAG系统模型输出的因果贡献，相比基线RAG-Ex在三个数据集上实现14.81%的解释质量提升（F1），增强了RAG系统的可解释性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG的推理过程是黑盒，现有面向文本检索的可解释性方法无法处理结构化知识组件间的关联，导致透明性和可信度存在关键缺口。

Method: 提出XGRAG框架，采用基于图的扰动策略，量化单个图组件（节点/边）对模型答案的因果贡献，生成因果归因解释。

Result: 在NarrativeQA、FairyTaleQA、TriviaQA上，XGRAG的解释质量（F1）比基线RAG-Ex提升14.81%；解释与图中心性度量强相关，验证了其捕捉图结构的能力。

Conclusion: XGRAG提供了一种可扩展、通用化的方法，通过透明、基于图的解释增强RAG系统的可解释性，推动可信AI发展。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) extends traditional RAG by using knowledge graphs (KGs) to give large language models (LLMs) a structured, semantically coherent context, yielding more grounded answers. However, GraphRAG reasoning process remains a black-box, limiting our ability to understand how specific pieces of structured knowledge influence the final output. Existing explainability (XAI) methods for RAG systems, designed for text-based retrieval, are limited to interpreting an LLM response through the relational structures among knowledge components, creating a critical gap in transparency and trustworthiness. To address this, we introduce XGRAG, a novel framework that generates causally grounded explanations for GraphRAG systems by employing graph-based perturbation strategies, to quantify the contribution of individual graph components on the model answer. We conduct extensive experiments comparing XGRAG against RAG-Ex, an XAI baseline for standard RAG, and evaluate its robustness across various question types, narrative structures and LLMs. Our results demonstrate a 14.81% improvement in explanation quality over the baseline RAG-Ex across NarrativeQA, FairyTaleQA, and TriviaQA, evaluated by F1-score measuring alignment between generated explanations and original answers. Furthermore, XGRAG explanations exhibit a strong correlation with graph centrality measures, validating its ability to capture graph structure. XGRAG provides a scalable and generalizable approach towards trustworthy AI through transparent, graph-based explanations that enhance the interpretability of RAG systems.

</details>


### [157] [The Price of Agreement: Measuring LLM Sycophancy in Agentic Financial Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24668)
*Zhenyu Zhao,Aparna Balagopalan,Adi Agrawal,Dilshoda Yergasheva,Waseem Alshikh,Daniel M. Bikel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在金融智能体任务中的谄媚行为，发现模型在面对用户反驳时性能下降不大，但面对与参考答案相矛盾的用户偏好信息时多数模型会失败，并基准测试了预训练LLM输入过滤等恢复模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在金融系统中的广泛应用，评估其安全性和鲁棒性变得至关重要。谄媚行为（模型优先同意用户表达的观点而非追求正确性）是LLM常见的失败模式，但该问题在金融领域尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 1) 评估模型在面对用户反驳或与参考答案相矛盾的用户偏好信息时的性能变化；2) 引入一套测试任务来检测由与参考答案相矛盾的用户偏好信息引发的谄媚行为；3) 基准测试多种恢复模式（如使用预训练LLM进行输入过滤）。

Result: 1) 模型在金融智能体任务中面对用户反驳时仅显示低到中等的性能下降，与先前工作中的发现不同；2) 当输入包含与参考答案相矛盾的用户偏好信息时，多数模型失败；3) 提供了不同恢复模式的基准测试结果。

Conclusion: 金融领域的LLM谄媚行为表现与通用领域存在差异，需要针对性的缓解策略。

Abstract: Given the increased use of LLMs in financial systems today, it becomes important to evaluate the safety and robustness of such systems. One failure mode that LLMs frequently display in general domain settings is that of sycophancy. That is, models prioritize agreement with expressed user beliefs over correctness, leading to decreased accuracy and trust. In this work, we focus on evaluating sycophancy that LLMs display in agentic financial tasks. Our findings are three-fold: first, we find the models show only low to modest drops in performance in the face of user rebuttals or contradictions to the reference answer, which distinguishes sycophancy that models display in financial agentic settings from findings in prior work. Second, we introduce a suite of tasks to test for sycophancy by user preference information that contradicts the reference answer and find that most models fail in the presence of such inputs. Lastly, we benchmark different modes of recovery such as input filtering with a pretrained LLM.

</details>


### [158] [Governing What You Cannot Observe: Adaptive Runtime Governance for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24686)
*German Marin,Jatin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出信息可行性原则与Agent可行性框架，通过监督(P1)、预期(P2)、单调限制(P3)三个必要条件保障自主AI Agent安全，并实现RiskGate参考系统及可行性指数VI(t)实现从反应式到预测式治理的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI Agent在无需代码变更的情况下，因行为漂移、对手适应及决策模式偏移而变得不安全，现有方法无法充分处理此类风险。

Method: 基于Aubin可行性理论，建立Agent可行性框架，提出信息可行性原则；设计RiskGate系统，包含KL散度、分段z检验等统计估计器、单调安全流水线及基于regulation map的闭环Autopilot，并引入可行性指数VI(t)实现一阶t*预测。

Result: 理论上证明监督、预期、单调限制三个属性是记录在案的失败模式各自必要且联合充分的；提供RiskGate参考实现及对已发布Agent失败分类的分析覆盖；定量实证评估留作后续工作。

Conclusion: 从理论、实现和分类覆盖三个层面贡献了自主Agent安全治理方案，将治理从反应式转变为预测式。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents can remain fully authorized and still become unsafe as behavior drifts, adversaries adapt, and decision patterns shift without any code change. We propose the \textbf{Informational Viability Principle}: governing an agent reduces to estimating a bound on unobserved risk $\hat{B}(x) = U(x) + SB(x) + RG(x)$ and allowing an action only when its capacity $S(x)$ exceeds $\hat{B}(x)$ by a safety margin. The \textbf{Agent Viability Framework}, grounded in Aubin's viability theory, establishes three properties -- monitoring (P1), anticipation (P2), and monotonic restriction (P3) -- as individually necessary and collectively sufficient for documented failure modes. \textbf{RiskGate} instantiates the framework with dedicated statistical estimators (KL divergence, segment-vs-rest $z$-tests, sequential pattern matching), a fail-secure monotonic pipeline, and a closed-loop Autopilot formalised as an instance of Aubin's regulation map with kill-switch-as-last-resort; a scalar Viability Index $VI(t) \in [-1,+1]$ with first-order $t^*$ prediction transforms governance from reactive to predictive. Contributions are the theoretical framework, the reference implementation, and analytical coverage against published agent-failure taxonomies; quantitative empirical evaluation is scoped as follow-up work.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Sliced Wasserstein Steering between Gaussian Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22807)
*Kaito Ito,Anqi Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于切片的分布控制反馈控制器，通过将高维分布投影到一维方向、求解最优一维速度并平均回高维空间，实现高效且可扩展的分布控制，并证明了在Gaussian情形下的收敛性及能量消耗与 sliced Wasserstein 距离的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间中基于最优传输的分布控制计算复杂且难以处理部分观测（如相机轮廓、激光雷达束、断层扫描切片），亟需一种可扩展且与线性观测兼容的反馈控制方法。

Method: 将演化分布投影到球面上的一维方向，在每个投影方向求解一维Benamou–Brenier问题得到最优速度，再将这些速度平均得到高维空间中的反馈控制律。该方法通过采样方向并行求解独立的一维子问题实现可扩展计算。

Result: 该控制器在正交变换下具有不变性、在投影下非扩张、在 $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^n)$ 上适定；在Gaussian设定下能成功将分布导向目标；推导出控制器能耗与 sliced Wasserstein 距离的恒等式。

Conclusion: 所提出的切片反馈控制器为高维分布控制提供了一种可扩展、与部分观测兼容的解决方案，兼具理论保证与计算可行性。

Abstract: Optimal transport with quadratic cost provides a geometric framework for steering an ensemble, modeled by a probability law, with minimal effort. Yet ambient-space formulations become unwieldy in high dimensions, and sensing or actuation in practice often reveals only linear views of the state -- camera silhouettes, LiDAR beams, tomographic slices. We develop a sliced feedback controller for distribution steering: the evolving law is projected onto one-dimensional directions on the sphere, the optimal one-dimensional velocity is synthesized in each projection, and these velocities are averaged to produce a feedback control in the ambient space. The construction reduces to the Benamou--Brenier problem in one dimension. In addition, it is invariant under orthogonal transforms, nonexpansive under projections, and well posed on $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^n)$. Computation proceeds by sampling directions on the sphere and solving independent one-dimensional subproblems, yielding a scalable method aligned with partial observations. In the Gaussian setting, we show that the developed sliced controller steers the law to the prescribed target. Furthermore, we derive an identity relating the energy consumption incurred by the controller to the sliced Wasserstein distance.

</details>


### [160] [Modeling Epidemic Spread with Strategic Vaccination and Socialization: a Mean Field Game Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22946)
*Huaning Liu,Gokce Dayanikli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模异质群体中流行病控制的博弈论模型，推导出描述平均场纳什均衡的正反向常微分方程组，证明均衡接种率具有至多一次跳跃的bang-bang结构，并通过数值实验验证了社交与接种的权衡以及隔离感染者比限制易感者更重要的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用博弈论框架分析非合作个体在流行病控制中关于社交水平和疫苗接种的联合决策，揭示个体理性行为下的均衡特征。

Method: 建立包含多个群体和非合作个体的流行病控制博弈模型，推导平均场纳什均衡的FBODE系统，证明bang-bang结构及Carathéodory解的存在性，并设计数值算法求解。

Result: 均衡接种率呈现至多一次跳跃的bang-bang结构；存在Carathéodory解；在考虑人口意识扩展下同样具有该性质；数值实验揭示了社交与接种的权衡关系，以及隔离感染者比限制易感者更有效。

Conclusion: 个体疫苗接种决策存在一个由模型参数决定的关键时间点，在此之前接种、之后停止；政策启示方面，资源应优先用于隔离感染者而非限制易感者。

Abstract: We study a game-theoretic model of epidemic control in a large population with finitely many groups and non-cooperative individuals. In the model, individuals jointly choose their socialization levels and vaccination rates, and vaccination is subject to a linear individual cost structure. We derive a forward-backward ordinary differential equations (FBODE) system that characterizes the mean field Nash equilibrium, show that the equilibrium vaccination rate exhibits an at-most one-jump bang-bang structure, and establish the existence of a Carathéodory solution to the FBODE. This establishes a realistic interpretation of the vaccination decisions, meaning individuals decide to vaccinate until a time point which is determined by model parameters and then stop after. We further consider a population-awareness extension in which individuals incorporate population infection information into their objective functions, and we prove a similar at-most one-jump bang-bang property under suitable conditions. Finally, we propose a numerical algorithm for solving the FBODE and conduct simulations to validate the theoretical findings. The experiments highlight two main insights: the trade-off between socialization and vaccination, and the greater importance of quarantining infected individuals instead of restricting susceptible individuals.

</details>


### [161] [A Reinforcement-learning-based Column Generation Algorithm for Integrated Operating Room Planning and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22977)
*Mahdi Dolatkhah,Hossein Hashemi Doulabi,Walter Rei,Michel Gendreau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合列生成、强化学习和遗传算法的混合整数规划模型，用于解决手术室排程问题。在合成数据和意大利那不勒斯医院真实数据上，平均最优性间隙分别为1.23%和1.49%，显著优于现有方法，并能处理大规模实例及手术时长不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术室排程方法在大规模实例上求解困难，且未充分考虑手术时长不确定性和紧急手术的影响。本文旨在提出一种高效、鲁棒的集成优化方法。

Method: 构建混合整数规划模型，考虑手术室加班和外科医生日可用时间限制；提出列生成算法求解大规模问题；为加速列生成，融合强化学习算法和遗传算法生成初始列；通过合成数据与真实医院数据验证模型有效性，并分析手术时长20%变异及紧急手术场景。

Result: 合成实例平均最优性间隙1.23%，真实场景1.49%；加入120分钟缓冲时间可最小化总成本；紧急手术使合成实例总重排成本增加4.13%，真实案例中优先级升级仅增加0.11%成本。

Conclusion: 所提方法在求解质量和鲁棒性上显著优于文献中现有方法，能有效处理大规模手术室排程问题，并为应对手术时长不确定性和紧急手术提供了可行策略。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel mixed integer programming model to formulate integrated operating room planning and scheduling problems, where several mandatory and elective surgeries are to be assigned and scheduled in operating rooms on different days. We consider both overtime in operating rooms and surgeons' daily availability limits. We propose a column generation (CG) algorithm to solve large-scale instances. In order to enhance the CG, we integrate the Reinforcement Learning Algorithm and the Genetic Algorithm and develop a hybrid algorithm to generate initial columns for the CG algorithm. For our analysis, we employed two sets of test instances: one consisting of synthetic data and the other based on real-world cases from a local hospital in Naples, Italy. Computational experiments demonstrate that our proposed model and methodology yields an average optimality gap of 1.23% for synthetic instances and 1.49% on real-world scenarios, significantly outperforming previous solution methodologies in the literature. Additionally, we demonstrate that the developed CG algorithm provides a high-quality solution for large-scale instances where other models and methods fail to obtain even a feasible solution. To further evaluate robustness under uncertainty, we examined scenarios with 20% variability in surgery durations. The results indicate that incorporating a 120-minute buffer time minimizes the overall cost. Moreover, we investigated the impact of emergency surgeries by either introducing additional cases or escalating surgical priorities. For synthetic instances, the inclusion of emergency surgeries increased the total rescheduling cost by 4.13%, whereas in the real-world Naples cases, priority escalation led to only a 0.11% increase.

</details>


### [162] [Computational Method for Desensitized Optimal Guidance Using Direct Collocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23028)
*Katrina Winkler,Anil Rao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用自适应高斯求积配点法的非敏感性最优制导方法。该方法通过增广目标函数显式惩罚状态对不确定参数的敏感性，生成参考轨迹；随后在指定制导更新时刻，采用Legendre-Gauss-Radau配点法求解缩小时域的最优控制问题，并通过网格重映射实现高效计算。在Zermelo导航问题和大气再入问题上的蒙特卡洛仿真表明，该方法能在不显著增加计算负担的前提下，获得更紧凑的轨迹包络和更小的终端状态误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优制导方法对动力学模型中的参数不确定性敏感，导致轨迹发散和终端误差增大。已有方法未有效兼顾鲁棒性与计算效率，因此需要一种既能显式降低灵敏度、又保持实时计算可行性的制导方案。

Method: 1) 增广目标函数，引入状态对不确定参数的灵敏度的显式惩罚项，求解非敏感性参考轨迹；2) 在每次制导更新时，将剩余时域上的最优控制问题用Legendre-Gauss-Radau配点法离散求解；3) 通过网格重映射（将原网格映射到剩余时域并删除过期部分）实现缩小时域网格的快速构建。

Result: Zermelo导航问题和大气再入问题中，相比不惩罚灵敏度的传统方法，该方法在蒙特卡洛仿真下产生更紧凑的轨迹包络和更小的终端状态误差，且计算负担未显著增加。

Conclusion: 提出的非敏感性最优制导方法能有效提升对外部扰动和建模误差的鲁棒性，同时保持计算效率，适用于存在参数不确定性的实际制导场景。

Abstract: A computational method is developed for desensitized optimal guidance using adaptive Gaussian quadrature collocation. The method computes a reference trajectory that reduces the sensitivity to uncertainties in the dynamic model by augmenting the objective functional to explicitly penalize the sensitivity of the state with respect to uncertain parameters. Using this desensitized reference trajectory as a starting point, the desensitized optimal guidance method developed in this paper computes a new optimal control on the remaining horizon at specified guidance update times. This shrinking horizon optimal control problem is solved using a Legendre-Gauss-Radau collocation method where, at each guidance update, a reduced-horizon mesh is determined by remapping the mesh to the remaining horizon and deleting the portion of the mesh associated with the expired portion of the horizon. The resulting guidance solution is found to improve robustness to external disturbances and modeling errors. The method is demonstrated on two numerical examples. The first example is Zermelo's navigation problem which illustrates the behavior of the method on a simple example. The second example is an atmospheric reentry problem that demonstrates the performance of the method on a more complex problem. For both examples, the dynamics are simulated in the presence of parameter uncertainties in the dynamic model, and Monte Carlo analysis is performed. The results show that the method developed in this paper produces tighter trajectory envelopes and smaller terminal state errors without significantly increasing the computational burden when compared with a method that does not penalize sensitivities.

</details>


### [163] [Nonlinear balanced truncation model reduction through scalable Taylor series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23044)
*Nicholas A. Corbin,Boris Kramer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多项式近似和张量积表示的非线性平衡截断可扩展算法，用于计算非线性系统的降阶模型，并通过实例展示了其优势与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性平衡截断理论为模型降阶提供了保持稳定性、可控性和可观性等关键属性的系统理论框架，但现有方法缺乏可扩展的计算算法，难以应用于实际大规模非线性系统。

Method: 基于多项式近似，利用Kronecker张量积表示，结合近期数值线性代数的进展，推导平衡变换和显式平衡实现的多项式近似，通过截断冗余状态分量得到非线性降阶模型。

Result: 在多个示例上测试了所提工具，展示了非线性平衡方法在现有文献中未充分体现的细致优缺点。

Conclusion: 所提算法实现了非线性平衡截断理论的可扩展计算，但实验结果也揭示了该方法在实际应用中的局限性，提供了更全面的视角。

Abstract: The theory of nonlinear balanced truncation provides a system-theoretic framework for model reduction that preserves important properties such as stability, controllability, and observability. We present a scalable algorithm for computing reduced-order models based on the nonlinear balancing theory. The approach is based on polynomial approximations using the Kronecker product representation, building on recent numerical linear algebra advancements to enable scalability. We derive polynomial approximations for the balancing transformation and the explicit balanced realization of the full-order model, which yields true nonlinear reduced-order models upon truncation of redundant state components. The proposed tools are tested on various examples, demonstrating a nuanced perspective of the benefits and limitations of nonlinear balancing not shown in the existing literature.

</details>


### [164] [Secure estimator design for Lur'e-type systems with nonuniformly and synchronously sampled measurements under attacks [extended version]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23217)
*Julian Gootzen,Michelle S. Chong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对电力配电网实时健康监测的需求，提出了一种针对Lur'e型连续时间系统的安全状态估计器设计方法，该估计器能在非均匀同步采样且部分传感器遭受恶意攻击的情况下，提供与攻击无关的状态估计精度，并通过脉冲系统框架证明了误差收敛性，给出了不依赖攻击信号的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力配电网需要实时健康监测，但传感器可能遭受恶意攻击，现有状态估计方法在攻击下性能退化，亟需一种能够抵抗传感器攻击并保证估计精度的安全状态估计器。

Method: 针对连续时间Lur'e型系统，考虑非均匀同步采样输出且部分传感器被恶意篡改的场景；在攻击传感器少于半数且采样间隔有上界的假设下，采用脉冲系统框架建模，通过线性矩阵不等式（LMI）设计观测器参数，并证明状态估计误差的收敛性及与攻击信号无关的上界。

Result: 所提安全状态估计器在少于半数传感器受攻击时，状态估计精度与传感器攻击无关；误差在脉冲系统框架下收敛，且误差上界不依赖攻击信号；在低压配电网上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的安全状态估计器能够有效抵御传感器攻击，适用于配电网实时监测场景，为不确定环境下电力系统的安全运行提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Motivated by the need for real-time health monitoring of power distribution grids, we propose a secure state estimator design for continuous time Lur'e type systems with non-uniformly and synchronously sampled outputs which have potentially been maliciously corrupted. The secure state estimator provides state estimates with accuracy independent of the sensor attack, when less than half of the sensors are under attack and when all inter-sample times are upper bounded. We show convergence of the state estimation error under an impulsive system framework and provide an upper bound on the estimation error that is independent of the attack signals. The stability conditions are formulated as linear matrix inequalities, which can be used to design the observer parameters. We demonstrate the capabilities of the proposed secure state estimator on a low-voltage power distribution grid.

</details>


### [165] [Nonlinear Non-Gaussian Density Steering with Input and Noise Channel Mismatch: Sinkhorn with Memory for Solving the Control-affine Schrödinger Bridge Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23370)
*Georgiy A. Bondar,Asmaa Eldesoukey,Yongxin Chen,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对控制仿射薛定谔桥问题中控制通道与噪声通道不匹配导致Hopf-Cole变换后PDE仍非线性、无法用标准Sinkhorn递归求解的困境，本文设计了一种带记忆的Sinkhorn递归算法，利用非线性PDE的结构特性实现求解，并证明了算法的局部稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准Sinkhorn递归求解薛定谔桥问题依赖于Hopf-Cole变换的线性化能力，但该线性化仅在控制通道与噪声通道成比例时成立。当两者不匹配时，变换后的PDE保持非线性，尚无有效算法可求解，限制了控制仿射薛定谔桥问题的应用范围。

Method: 设计了一种带记忆的Sinkhorn递归算法，利用控制仿射薛定谔桥问题中非线性PDE的结构特性，在递归过程中引入记忆机制来克服通道不匹配带来的非线性困难。

Result: 成功求解了控制与噪声通道不匹配下的控制仿射薛定谔桥问题，并证明了所提算法的局部稳定性。

Conclusion: 带记忆的Sinkhorn递归有效突破了传统方法对通道匹配条件的依赖，为更一般化的最优密度控制问题提供了可行的数值求解方案。

Abstract: Solutions to the Schrödinger bridge problem and its generalizations yield feedback control policies for optimal density steering over a controlled diffusion. To numerically compute the same, the dynamic Sinkhorn recursion has become a standard approach. The mathematical engine behind this approach is the Hopf-Cole transform that recasts the conditions for optimality into a system of boundary-coupled linear PDEs. Recent works pointed out that for the control-affine Schrödinger bridge problem, this exact linearity via Hopf-Cole transform, and thus the standard Sinkhorn recursion, apply only if the control and noise channels are proportional. When the channels do not match, the Hopf-Cole-transformed PDEs remain nonlinear, and no algorithm is available to solve the same. We advance the state-of-the-art by designing a Sinkhorn recursion with memory that leverages the structure of these nonlinear PDEs, and demonstrate how it solves the control-affine Schrödinger bridge problem with input and noise channel mismatch. We prove the local stability of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [166] [Probabilistic analysis of dual decomposition on two-stage stochastic integer programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23383)
*Santanu S. Dey,Marco Molinaro,Jingye Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对分支定价（Branch-and-Price）算法在二阶段随机二元整数规划问题上的平均情形复杂度进行了首次分析，证明了在随机输入模型下，搜索树大小以高概率达到拟多项式界n^O(log s)，同时揭示了线性规划松弛的整数性间隙以O((log s log^2 n)/n)速率收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分解算法（如Benders方法和分支定价）在实际中表现优异，但理论分析几乎完全基于最坏情形，预测指数级收敛行为，无法解释实际中的强性能表现。本文旨在填补理论与实践的鸿沟，提供平均情形下的理论保证。

Method: 采用随机输入模型，其中目标系数和约束矩阵随机生成，右端向量随决策维度缩放，每场景约束数固定。基于此模型，对分支定价算法进行平均情形分析，关键步骤是证明线性规划松弛的整数性间隙在平均意义下的收缩速率。

Result: 1) 分支定价算法以高概率最多探索n^O(log s)个节点，即搜索树大小为拟多项式界；2) 线性规划松弛的整数性间隙以O((log s log^2 n)/n)速率收缩，且该收缩仅随场景数s对数增长。

Conclusion: 本文首次为分支定价算法在随机二阶段随机整数规划上的高效性能提供了理论依据，整数性间隙的平均情形界具有独立的理论价值，表明实际中该问题远不如最坏情形所预测的那么困难。

Abstract: Two-stage stochastic integer programs provide a powerful framework for modeling decision-making under uncertainty, but they are notoriously difficult to solve at scale due to their high dimensionality and intrinsic nonconvexity. Decomposition-based algorithms such as Benders methods and Branch-and-Price (related dual decomposition methods) have become standard computational approaches for such problems and demonstrate excellent empirical performance in practice. Despite their widespread use, however, existing theoretical guarantees are almost exclusively based on worst-case analyses, which predict exponential convergence behavior in the problem dimension and fail to explain the strong performance observed in practice. In this paper, we present the first average-case analysis of Branch-and-Price for a broad class of two-stage stochastic binary integer programs. We study a stochastic-input model in which objective coefficients and constraint matrices are drawn at random and right-hand-side vectors scale with the decision dimension, while the number of constraints per scenario is fixed. Under this model, we prove that, with high probability, Branch-and-Price explores at most n^O(log s)nodes, yielding a quasi-polynomial bound on the size of the search tree in typical instances, where n denotes the decision dimension and s the number of scenarios. A key ingredient of our analysis is an average-case bound on the integrality gap of the natural linear programming (LP) relaxation. We show that this gap shrinks at rate O((logs log^2 n)/n)with high probability. This result is of independent interest, as it implies that the integrality gap grows only logarithmically with the number of scenarios on average.

</details>


### [167] [Vertical Control Systems on Tangent Bundles and Fiberwise Controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23414)
*S. N. Stelmastchuk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究切丛上由向量场垂直提升诱导的控制系统，刻画了纤维可控性的秩条件，并通过传输算子给出了含漂移的 lifted 系统的可控性充要条件及李代数充分判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解切丛上由向量场垂直提升控制的系统动力学，特别是纤维方向的可控性，并扩展到含完整漂移的 lifted 系统。

Method: 通过向量场的垂直提升将系统分解为纤维上的线性控制问题，利用传输算子分析漂移加垂直控制的 lifted 系统。

Result: 证明了纤维可控性等价于原始向量场的秩条件；对lifted系统导出了显式解和可控性的充要条件及李代数充分判据。

Conclusion: 建立了切丛上垂直提升控制系统的可控性理论框架，为更复杂的几何控制问题提供了分析工具。

Abstract: We study control systems on the tangent bundle of a smooth manifold induced by vertical lifts of vector fields. The Vertical dynamics acts exclusively along the fibers, leaving the base point unchanged and reducing the system to a linear control problem on each tangent space, for which we obtain explicit solutions and characterize reachable sets, showing that fiberwise controllability is equivalent to a rank condition on the original vector fields. We then consider lifted systems combining complete drift and vertical controls, where the base trajectory is fixed by the drift and the control acts on tangent directions. For these systems, we derive explicit solutions and a complete characterization of reachable sets via a transport operator, yielding a necessary and sufficient condition for fiberwise controllability in terms of transported vector fields, together with a Lie-algebraic sufficient criterion.

</details>


### [168] [Optimality Conditions and Numerical Algorithms for a Class of Minimax Bilevel Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23487)
*Yaling Hu,Jiani Wang,Yu-hong Dai,Xiaojiao Tong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的极小极大双层优化问题，建立了其最优性条件，并开发了高效的一阶算法（投影梯度多步上升下降法及其Nesterov加速版本），能在$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3} \log(ε^{-1}))$迭代内找到ε-KKT解，并在电力系统双层经济调度等任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用（如Stackelberg博弈、机器学习、电力系统）中，极小极大优化问题的决策受到另一个优化问题解的约束，现有研究缺乏对此类极小极大双层优化问题的系统性理论与高效算法。

Method: (1) 通过KKT条件和值函数重构下层问题，建立最优性条件；(2) 提出惩罚方法框架，将双层问题转化为单层极小极大问题；(3) 设计投影梯度多步上升下降法及其Nesterov加速扩展，迭代复杂度均为$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3} \log(ε^{-1}))$。

Result: 所提算法能在$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3} \log(ε^{-1}))$迭代次数内找到原问题的ε-KKT解；Nesterov加速版本在相同复杂度下提升了收敛速度；数值实验验证了模型与算法在多种极小极大双层优化问题及电力系统双层经济调度中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为极小极大双层优化问题奠定了理论基础，提供了高效的求解算法，并展示了其在电力系统等实际场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: In many applications, including Stackelberg games, machine learning, and power systems \cite{Mackay2018Selftuning,Heinrich1952The,Wang2021Bi-Level}, the decisions in a minimax optimization problem can be constrained by a solution to an optimization problem. In this paper, we introduce optimality conditions of this novel minimax bilevel optimization problem and develops efficient first-order algorithms for this class of problems. Firstly, we establish the optimality conditions for minimax bilevel problems by reconstructing the lower-level problem through its Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions and value function. Secondly, we develop a penalty method framework to approximately solve the minimax bilevel problem by transforming it into a single-level minimax problem. Thirdly, we design a projected gradient multi-step ascent descent method to solve the resulting minimax problem, which can find an $ε$-KKT solution for the original minimax bilevel problem within $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3} \log(ε^{-1}))$ iterations. To improve {the convergence rate} of the algorithm, we provide its Nesterov accelerated extension with $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3} \log(ε^{-1}))$ iteration complexity. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our model and algorithms through numerical experiments on various minimax bilevel optimization problems and a bilevel economic dispatch in the power system.

</details>


### [169] [Wireless Mobile Charging for Emergency Electric Vehicle Routing: A Mixed-Integer and Metaheuristic Framework for In-Motion Energy Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23561)
*Jingyi Zhao,Haoxiang Yang,Youxuan Pan,Yang Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出无线移动充电电动汽车路径问题（WMC-EVRP），通过感应耦合技术在行驶中为电动汽车无线充电，并开发了混合位姿动态规划与大邻域搜索算法，在新加坡医院物流数据上验证了显著优于商业求解器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定或移动充电基础设施需要车辆停驶，不适用于器官运输等非停运紧急物流场景，亟需一种支持车辆在运动中持续供电的充电方案。

Method: 构建混合整数规划模型联合优化异构车队路径与动态无线能量传输；设计混合位姿动态规划与大邻域搜索算法处理弧级充电决策与双车队同步。

Result: 基于新加坡真实医院物流数据的实验表明，所提算法在求解时间和解质量上均显著优于商业求解器。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了含时-空依赖动态移动能量传输的计算电力系统建模，为规划移动充电基础设施、增强需求侧灵活性及提升应急物流韧性提供了可操作见解。

Abstract: As electric vehicles (EVs) become central to decarbonization efforts, the need for uninterrupted power supply in time-critical logistics, particularly in medical transportation, poses unique challenges for power systems integration. Conventional fixed or mobile charging infrastructure requires vehicle downtime, which makes them unsuitable for nonstop operations such as organ delivery. This work introduces the Wireless Mobile Charging Electric Vehicle Routing Problem, a novel framework in which mobile charging trucks wirelessly transfer energy to moving EVs via inductive coupling, eliminating the need for stationary charging stops. We formulate a mixed-integer programming model that co-optimizes routing and in-motion energy transfer between heterogeneous vehicle fleets under temporal and spatial alignment constraints. To address computational complexity, we develop a hybrid Bitmask Dynamic Programming and Large Neighborhood Search algorithm, tailored to arc-level charging decisions and dual-fleet synchronization. Experiments using real-world hospital logistics data from Singapore demonstrate significant runtime improvements and higher-quality solutions compared with commercial solvers. This study advances computational power system modeling by incorporating dynamic, motion-based energy delivery that is both time- and space-dependent. The results provide actionable insights into planning mobile energy infrastructure, enhancing demand-side flexibility, strengthening resilience in emergency logistics, and integrating wireless charging into urban power systems to support flexible and zero-carbon electrified mobility.

</details>


### [170] [High-Probability Guarantees for Random Zeroth-Order (Stochastic) Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23613)
*Haishan Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑强凸目标函数，建立了随机零阶梯度下降法在高概率意义下的收敛保证。在确定性和随机两种设定下，分别给出了达到ε-次优解所需的函数查询次数的上界，其中置信参数仅以对数形式出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典零阶优化方法主要在期望意义下被理解，但其高概率行为（尤其是在光滑强凸目标下）所知甚少。实际应用中需要可靠的高概率性能保证，因此有必要建立高概率收敛理论。

Method: 针对确定性L-光滑μ-强凸d维目标，分析经典二次查询随机零阶梯度下降法的高概率收敛性；针对随机目标，在有限噪声条件下（不假设随机梯度一致有界），分析随机零阶随机梯度下降法的高概率行为。

Result: 确定性情形：O((dL/μ)log(1/ε) + log(1/δ))次查询；随机情形：O(d log(1/ε)(log(1/ε)+log(1/δ))/ε)次查询。相比期望意义下的复杂度，高概率保证仅增加关于置信参数的对数依赖。

Conclusion: 本文为经典期望意义下的零阶收敛保证提供了高概率对应版本，阐明了获得可靠性能保证所需的额外代价，表明置信参数仅以对数形式影响复杂度。

Abstract: Zeroth-order optimization aims to minimize an objective function using only function evaluations, and is therefore fundamental in black-box optimization, hyperparameter tuning, bandit learning, and adversarial machine learning. While classical zeroth-order methods are well understood in expectation, much less is known about their high-probability behavior, especially for smooth and strongly convex objectives. In this paper, we establish high-probability convergence guarantees for random zeroth-order gradient descent in both deterministic and stochastic settings. For deterministic $L$-smooth and $μ$-strongly convex objectives of $d$-dimension, we show that the classical two-query random zeroth-order method finds an $\varepsilon$-suboptimal solution with probability at least $1-δ$ using
  \[
  \mathcal{O}\left(
  \frac{dL}μ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}
  +
  \log\frac{1}δ
  \right)
  \]
  function queries. Thus, compared with the standard in-expectation complexity, only an additive logarithmic dependence on the confidence parameter is needed. For stochastic objectives, under a bounded-noise condition and without assuming uniformly bounded stochastic gradients, we prove that random zeroth-order stochastic gradient descent achieves an $\varepsilon$-suboptimal solution with probability at least $1-δ$ using
  \[
  \mathcal{O}\left(
  \frac{
  d\log(1/\varepsilon)
  \left(\log(1/\varepsilon)+\log(1/δ)\right)
  }{\varepsilon}
  \right)
  \]
  queries. Our results provide high-confidence counterparts to classical expectation-based zeroth-order convergence guarantees and clarify the additional cost required to obtain reliable performance guarantees.

</details>


### [171] [On Tikhonov Regularization for Direct and Indirect Data-Driven LQR Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23650)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zheming Wang,Raphael M. Jungers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于正则化协方差参数化的直接数据驱动LQR控制新方法，该方法在处理病态数据矩阵时依然有效，并等价于结合Tikhonov正则化的间接确定性等价LQR，同时可扩展至基于Koopman线性嵌入的非线性系统控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接数据驱动控制中正则化不仅隐式实现系统辨识，还对确保闭环可靠性至关重要；现有方法在处理病态情况（如数据矩阵条件数大）时效果不佳，需要更鲁棒的方案。

Method: 提出基于正则化协方差参数化的直接数据驱动LQR控制方法，证明了该方法与结合Tikhonov正则化的间接确定性等价LQR的等价性，并将该方法通过Koopman线性嵌入扩展至非线性系统。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提正则化方法的有效性和优越性，特别是在处理病态数据矩阵时的鲁棒性优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 新的正则化协方差参数化方法为直接数据驱动LQR控制提供了更可靠的方案，且理论等价性和非线性扩展路径清晰，具有较好的应用前景。

Abstract: In recent years, the so-called `direct data-driven control' has been a topic of intense research, and it is expected that it will become prominent in future complex dynamical systems control. Within this framework, regularization not only implicitly enforces system identification, but also plays a crucial role in ensuring reliable closed-loop behavior. To further enhance the performance of data-driven controllers, we propose a new regularization method for direct data-driven LQR control of unknown LTI systems, based on a regularized covariance parameterization. Unlike existing data-driven techniques, the proposed method remains effective in handling ill-conditioned cases, such as when the data matrix has a large condition number. Then, we demonstrate that our method is equivalent to the indirect certainty-equivalence LQR combined with Tikhonov regularization. Furthermore, we extend our method to the design of controllers for unknown nonlinear systems using Koopman linear embedding. Finally, the simulation results validate the effectiveness and advantages of the proposed regularization method.

</details>


### [172] [Enforcing TSP-Optimality in Fair Vehicle Routing by Cutting Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23748)
*Bart van Rossum,Rui Chen,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了公平容量约束车辆路径问题，目标是最小化最长与最短路径的差值（range）。由于range目标非单调，现有方法效率低下。作者提出分支-定价-切割框架，通过TSP最优性切割和提升技术，在25个客户规模的实例上实现了接近最优的求解（平均差距0.27%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平容量约束车辆路径问题中，range目标非单调，通过人为延长路径可降低range，导致违反单条路径的TSP最优性。现有精确方法难以高效处理这一挑战。

Method: 提出分支-定价-切割（branch-price-and-cut）框架，引入TSP最优性切割（TSP-optimality cuts）以禁止TSP支配的弧序列，并通过专门提升（lifting）程序加强切割效果。

Result: 在25个客户的基准实例上，该方法几乎求解了所有实例的最优解，在最困难配置上的平均最优性差距为0.27%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效解决公平容量约束车辆路径问题，验证了TSP最优性切割结合提升策略在非单调目标下的有效性。

Abstract: We study the fair capacitated vehicle routing problem, in which a fleet of vehicles must serve a set of customers such that the difference between the longest and shortest route, the range, is minimized. A key challenge is that the range objective is non-monotonic: it can be reduced by artificially lengthening routes, leading to solutions that violate TSP-optimality of individual routes. Existing exact methods struggle to handle this efficiently. We propose a branch-price-and-cut framework that enforces TSP-optimality through TSP-optimality cuts, which forbid TSP-dominated arc sequences. We strengthen the cuts through a dedicated lifting procedure. Computational experiments on benchmark instances with up to 25 customers show the method solves nearly all instances to optimality, achieving an average gap of 0.27% on the hardest configurations.

</details>


### [173] [Regret-Optimal Control for Finite-State Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23760)
*Yishay Polatov,Oron Sabag*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限状态系统在外部扰动下的控制问题，提出一种基于动态遗憾的基准相对框架，利用嵌套动态规划求解最优最坏情况遗憾与遗憾最优策略，数值实验表明该策略能在无需扰动分布知识的情况下，在MDP与鲁棒控制之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MDP方法假设扰动独立同分布，鲁棒控制方法则针对最坏情况优化，两者均存在局限。作者希望提出一种基准相对框架，使得在有利扰动下对标强基准、在不利扰动下考虑基准性能下降，从而更灵活地平衡性能与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出动态遗憾形式化目标，引入Regret-Bellman算子，通过其不动点值函数构建有限时域动态规划，同时计算最优最坏情况遗憾与遗憾最优策略。

Result: 遗憾最优策略在无需扰动分布知识的情况下，能够自然地在MDP策略与鲁棒控制策略之间插值；在独立同分布或结构化扰动下，甚至能同时优于两者。

Conclusion: 该基准相对框架为无模型控制提供了一种新的有效范式，弥补了MDP与鲁棒控制之间的空白。

Abstract: We study the control of finite-state systems driven by exogenous disturbances, and design causal policies that track the performance of a lookahead benchmark controller. This objective is formalized through dynamic regret, so that favorable disturbance sequences are compared against a strong benchmark, while under adverse disturbance sequences the comparison accounts for the benchmark's degraded performance. This benchmark-relative framework provides an alternative to classical MDP formulations, which assume i.i.d. disturbances, and to robust control approaches, which optimize against worst-case disturbances. Our main result is a nested dynamic-programming solution that computes both the optimal worst-case regret and a regret-optimal policy. In particular, we introduce the Regret-Bellman operator, whose fixed-point value function feeds into a finite-horizon dynamic program. Numerical examples show that regret-optimal policies interpolate nicely between MDP-based and robust controllers without requiring knowledge of the disturbance distribution, and can even outperform both under i.i.d. or structured disturbances.

</details>


### [174] [Learning to Control Stabilization in Column Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23889)
*Olivia Wang,Reem Khir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架来理解列生成中的平滑和惩罚稳定化技术，揭示两者都受参考点选择和稳定化参数控制，并基于此设计了一个强化学习引导的自适应参数选择框架RLSCG，在切割库存问题上显著减少了求解时间和迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列生成算法常因初始对偶估计不佳和对偶振荡导致收敛缓慢，现有平滑和惩罚等稳定化技术虽有效但严重依赖参数调优，缺乏自适应的参数选择机制。

Method: 首先建立平滑和惩罚的统一框架，推导保证进展的参数边界并给出收敛性证明；然后基于此设计RLSCG框架，利用强化学习在每次迭代中自适应选择稳定化参数。

Result: 在切割库存问题的合成和基准实例上，RLSCG相比传统列生成、基于规则的自适应稳定化和基于学习的列选择方法，显著降低了求解时间和迭代次数，尤其在大规模实例上提升最大。

Conclusion: 统一框架揭示了稳定化技术的核心设计维度，RLSCG验证了强化学习自适应参数选择的有效性，为列生成算法的加速提供了新思路。

Abstract: Column generation is a widely used decomposition technique for large-scale linear programs, but it often suffers from slow convergence due to poor initial dual estimates and dual oscillations. Stabilization techniques such as smoothing and penalization can mitigate these issues, but their effectiveness depends heavily on parameter selection, which requires careful tuning to avoid degrading performance. This paper presents a common framework for smoothing and penalization, showing that despite their different mechanisms, both are governed by two design choices: a reference point in the dual space and stabilization parameters that regulate how strongly that reference influences pricing. Within this framework, we derive parameter bounds that ensure progress, analyze predicted duals as reference points, and establish convergence guarantees for both methods. These results motivate and guide the design of RLSCG, a reinforcement learning-guided framework that adaptively selects stabilization parameters at each iteration. Computational experiments on the Cutting Stock Problem show that RLSCG substantially reduces solution time and iteration count on most synthetic and benchmark instances relative to traditional column generation, rule-based adaptive stabilization, and learning-based column selection, with the largest gains on large-scale instances.

</details>


### [175] [Isospectral Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23895)
*Ralph Sabbagh,Tryphon T. Georgiou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在等谱旋转约束下（增益矩阵特征值固定，仅调制特征向量）微分Lyapunov方程的可控性，刻画了任意终端时间和特征值设定下协方差的可达集，理论涉及多重线性代数、正线性代数及Birkhoff-von Neumann定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源于一类旨在减少各向异性变形的控制目标（最小剪切/注意力），当群体由共同定律引导时，最优性要求特征值保持恒定，从而引出了等谱转向问题。

Method: 通过对称时变增益矩阵（特征值固定，仅调制特征向量）对线性梯度场进行等谱旋转控制，利用多重线性代数、正线性代数以及双随机矩阵的Birkhoff-von Neumann定理分析微分Lyapunov方程的可控性。

Result: 描述了任意指定终端时间和增益矩阵特征值设定下，协方差的可达集。

Conclusion: 本文提出并论证了等谱转向控制问题的理论基础，建立了与多重线性代数和正线性代数的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study the controllability of the differential Lyapunov equation under isospectral rotation of a linear gradient field. Specifically, control is effected by a symmetric time-varying gain-matrix constrained to have fixed eigenvalues; that is, by exclusively modulating the eigen-vectors of the state matrix and not its eigenvalues. Motivation for this problem stems from a certain type of control objectives (minimum shear/attention) aimed to reduce anisotropic deformation when ensembles are steered by a common law--optimality necessitates constancy of eigenvalues. In the paper we introduce and motivate this type of isospectral steering, and describe the reachable set of covariances for any specified terminal time and eigenvalues of the gain matrix. The theory we develop is intimately linked to multilinear algebra as well as to positive linear algebra and the Birkoff-von Neumann theorem for doubly stochastic matrices.

</details>


### [176] [Presolving for GPU-Accelerated First-Order LP Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23951)
*Daniel Cederberg,Stephen Boyd*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了GPU加速线性规划求解器中的预求解环节，提出轻量级预求解方法PSLP，能够在保持与Gurobi商业求解器相当的问题缩减效果的同时大幅降低计算开销，为cuPDLPx等GPU加速求解器带来显著的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU加速一阶LP求解器研究忽视了预求解的作用，而传统CPU预求解器假设预求解时间不占主导，倾向于采用昂贵规则最大化问题缩减。在高性能GPU加速求解器背景下，这种传统预求解方式是否适用尚不明确。

Method: 识别一组相对简单的预求解规则，精心工程化集成；开发开源C语言实现的LP预求解器PSLP。

Result: PSLP以极低的计算成本实现了与Gurobi商业预求解器相当的问题缩减效果；在GPU加速求解器cuPDLPx中，尽管预求解有时占总运行时间的显著比例，仍能带来实质性端到端加速。

Conclusion: 轻量级预求解在GPU性能持续扩展的背景下仍然有益，而预求解的序列化特性不会成为瓶颈。PSLP已被社区采纳并集成到cuPDLPx、cuOpt和HPR-LP中。

Abstract: Recent research has focused on developing GPU-accelerated first-order solvers for linear programming (LP). This line of work, however, has largely overlooked the role of presolving, and thus prior results do not fully reflect the speedups achievable through GPU acceleration in a realistic end-to-end solver pipeline. At the same time, LP presolving has traditionally been developed for CPU-based solvers, where presolve time rarely dominates the total runtime and the emphasis has been on maximizing the reduction in problem size, even at the expense of costly presolve rules. Given the high performance of modern GPU-accelerated solvers and the inherently sequential nature of presolving, it is unclear whether this traditional approach to presolving remains appropriate.
  In this paper we revisit LP presolving from the perspective of GPU-accelerated first-order LP solvers. We identify a set of relatively simple presolve rules and show that a carefully engineered collection of these captures most of the reduction achieved by Gurobi's commercial state-of-the-art presolver, at a fraction of the cost. Moreover, we demonstrate that such lightweight presolving can yield substantial end-to-end speedups for the GPU-accelerated solver cuPDLPx, despite presolving sometimes constituting a significant fraction of the total runtime. These results suggest that lightweight presolving may remain beneficial as GPU performance continues to scale, while the sequential nature of presolving presumably does not.
  We accompany this paper with an open-source C implementation of an LP presolver, called PSLP (Presolver for Linear Programs). PSLP is battle-tested and has been adopted by the community, with integrations in cuPDLPx, cuOpt (NVIDIA's optimization library), and HPR-LP.

</details>


### [177] [SUDA-Muon: Structural Design Principles and Boundaries for Fully Decentralized Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23980)
*Hengrui Zhang,Boao Kong,Jiahe Geng,Zhengyang Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出SUDA统一通信模板，将去中心化Muon优化器解耦为模块化通信骨架与非模块化操作，证明了拓扑无关的收敛保证，并识别出两种非模块化边界（内部：跟踪先于极化；外部：局部极化后平均），实验验证了所提方法在非IID场景下的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化Muon因非线性矩阵符号算子与线性gossip平均不交换而难以实现，需要区分模块化组件与非模块化组件来设计算法结构。

Method: 提出SUDA（统一原-对偶通信模板），在该框架下ED/D²、EXTRA和梯度跟踪成为模块化骨干选择；通过拓扑分离的非渐近收敛分析证明主导项为O((1+σ/√N)K^{-1/4})且不显式包含图量；识别内部边界（无跟踪变体需跟踪先于极化以避免异构目标下的非平稳不动点）和外部边界（全去中心化方法不能执行联邦平均后极化更新，局部极化后平均设计无法实现线性加速）。

Result: 理论层面：证明了核范数几何下拓扑分离的收敛保证；识别了两种互补的非模块化边界。实验层面：在CIFAR-100和GPT-2微调上，统一模板使不同通信算法可直接比较；近IID场景各变体表现相似，长时程非IID CIFAR-100设置下SudaMuon比DeMuon精度更高、损失更低。

Conclusion: SUDA统一模板有效解构了去中心化Muon的设计空间，通信骨干是结构设计中的模块化轴；内部非模块化边界要求跟踪先于极化，外部非模块化边界揭示了全去中心化方法无法实现联邦式平均后极化的本质原因。

Abstract: Fully decentralized Muon is difficult because its nonlinear matrix-sign operator does not commute with linear gossip averaging. This makes decentralized Muon a structural design problem: in designing the algorithm, one must distinguish modular components from non-modular ones. We propose \sudamuon{}, which realizes this separation through a unified primal--dual communication template called SUDA; within this template, ED/D$^2$, EXTRA, and gradient tracking become modular backbone choices. We prove a topology-separated non-asymptotic convergence guarantee in the nuclear-norm geometry: the dominant term scales as $\mathcal{O}((1+σ/\sqrt{N})K^{-1/4})$ and does not explicitly involve graph quantities, identifying the communication backbone as the modular axis in the structure design. We then establish two complementary non-modular boundaries. Internally, tracking-before-polarization is necessary for this natural no-tracking variant to avoid non-stationary fixed points under heterogeneous objectives. Externally, in the absence of a central server, a fully decentralized method cannot perform the federated average-then-polarize update; we show that this non-modular local-polarize-then-average design is the essential reason why can fail to exhibit linear speedup. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and GPT-2 fine-tuning support the same picture: the unified template makes different communication algorithms directly comparable. In mild near-IID regimes, the resulting variants perform similarly, while in the more difficult long-horizon non-IID CIFAR-100 setting, \sudamuon{} achieves higher accuracy and lower loss than \textsc{DeMuon}.

</details>


### [178] [An online adaptive finite-element method for nonsmooth PDE-constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24065)
*Harbir Antil,Robert J. Baraldi,Rohit Khandelwal,Drew P. Kouri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信赖域的 adaptive 有限元算法，用于求解包含稀疏正则化和凸约束的非光滑 PDE 约束优化问题，通过自适应网格细化和后验误差估计器在计算代价与求解精度间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理非光滑 PDE 约束优化问题时，要么鲁棒性不足，要么计算效率低下，需要一种兼具鲁棒性和自适应离散化效率的统一框架。

Method: 将 inexact 信赖域算法（处理非光滑目标函数）与 adaptive 有限元方法（从粗网格开始，基于状态方程和伴随方程的后验误差估计器自动细化网格）相结合，系统控制光滑目标函数值和梯度的计算精度。

Result: 通过控制优化和拓扑优化的代表性数值实验验证了算法在解析局部现象和稀疏结构方面的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效解决一类含稀疏正则化和凸约束的非光滑 PDE 约束优化问题，在计算效率和解的精度之间实现良好平衡。

Abstract: We present a trust-region-based adaptive finite-element algorithm for numerically solving a class of nonsmooth PDE-constrained optimization problems that includes problems with sparsifying regularizers and convex constraints. In particular, we consider the class of problems whose objective function is the sum of a smooth, possibly nonconvex, function and a nonsmooth extended real-valued convex function. Our method combines the robustness of inexact trust-region algorithms for nonsmooth problems with the efficiency of adaptive finite-element discretizations. Starting from a coarse mesh, the algorithm automatically refines the discretization based on reliable a posteriori error estimators for both the state and adjoint equations, systematically controlling the accuracy of the computed smooth objective function value and gradient. This adaptivity mechanism balances computational cost and solution accuracy, enabling high resolution of localized phenomena and sparsity structures in the state and control variables. We demonstrate the performance of our algorithm through numerical experiments on representative control and topology optimization examples.

</details>


### [179] [Optimal control for a fourth-order nonisothermal tumor growth model of Caginalp type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24108)
*Cavalleri Giulia,Pierluigi Colli,Elisabetta Rocca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究热疗中肿瘤生长的非等温Caginalp型相场模型的分布式最优控制问题，证明了最优控制的存在性，推导了必要的一阶最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为肿瘤热疗中的温度控制提供数学理论基础，通过最优控制方法优化热疗过程，实现对肿瘤生长的有效调控。

Method: 建立耦合Cahn-Hilliard方程、热平衡方程和养分反应扩散方程的PDE系统，引入追踪型代价泛函，利用变分分析和伴随状态方法推导最优控制的存在性与一阶必要条件。

Result: 证明了最优控制的存在性，建立了控制-状态算子的可微性，并通过伴随状态变量的变分不等式给出了必要的一阶最优性条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为热疗中肿瘤生长的最优温度控制提供了严格的数学分析框架，验证了理论方法的可行性。

Abstract: We study a distributed optimal control problem for a nonisothermal Caginalp-type phase-field model that describes tumour growth under thermal therapy. The PDE system couples a possibly viscous Cahn-Hilliard equation, governing the evolution of the healthy and tumor phases, with an equation for the heat balance, and a reaction-diffusion equation for the nutrient concentration. Chemotaxis and active transport effects are taken into account, and hyperthermia appears as a control variable. We introduce a suitable tracking-type cost functional and show the existence of optimal controls. Then, we analyse the differentiability of the control-to-state operator and establish necessary first-order conditions expressed through a variational inequality involving the adjoint state variables.

</details>


### [180] [The Benjamin-Ono equation with 2D control input: approximate controllability and its application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24130)
*Jia-Cheng Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了环面上非线性Benjamin-Ono方程通过二维控制输入的近似可控性，并研究了随机强迫方程的长时间动力学，证明了在非退化且时间周期统计的随机力作用下，轨迹几乎必然在Sobolev范数下无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性Benjamin-Ono方程的控制性质及其在随机强迫下的长时间动力学行为。

Method: 基于Agrachev和Sarychev引入的几何控制方法的适应与推广。

Result: 1) 建立了环面上非线性Benjamin-Ono方程通过二维控制输入的近似可控性；2) 证明了在非退化且时间周期统计的随机力作用下，轨迹在Sobolev范数下几乎必然无界。

Conclusion: 非线性Benjamin-Ono方程具有近似可控性，且随机强迫下的解在Sobolev范数下几乎必然爆破（无界）。

Abstract: We establish the approximate controllability in $L^2$ for the nonlinear Benjamin-Ono equation on torus via two-dimensional control input. Our proof is based on adaptations of geometric control approach introduced by Agrachev and Sarychev. As an application of this control result, we study long-time dynamics of a randomly forced equation. It is proved that the trajectories are unbounded in Sobolev norms almost surely, when the random force is nondegenerate and statistically periodic in time.

</details>


### [181] [Heterogeneous-Horizon Exact-Weight Local SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24463)
*Dmitry Pasechnyuk-Vilensky,Martin Takáč,Eduard Gorbunov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对凸有限和优化中的异构本地SGD，提出HEW-Local SGD方法，通过最小化单步上界自适应选择服务器权重，支持异构本地轮数、小批量大小、梯度噪声和参与度，并建立了单步保证和全局收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地SGD方法通常假设同构设置（各节点使用相同的本地轮数、批量大小等），而实际联邦学习场景中客户端异构性普遍存在（如计算资源、数据分布差异）。需要一种能自适应处理异构本地轮数、小批量大小、梯度噪声和参与度的聚合方法。

Method: 提出HEW-Local SGD方法：通过最小化下一步目标值的显式单轮上界来选择节点级服务器权重，形成带阈值单纯形更新的精确本地控制框架，支持可分离的幅度更新，并在任意可预测参与模式下具有单步保证。同时提出两种后本地变体：修正的异构方法和更简单的同构特化版本。

Result: 建立了单步保证和全局基准风格的收敛结果。在可比区间内，理论定性与近期LocalSGD/SCAFFOLD分析的通信高效图像相匹配，同时给出了不等本地轮数下的显式保证。

Conclusion: HEW-Local SGD能有效处理异构本地SGD中的多种异质性来源，其理论结果与现有最优方法定性一致，并为异构本地轮数提供了显式收敛保证，拓展了本地SGD方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We study adaptive aggregation for heterogeneous local SGD in convex finite-sum optimization, allowing heterogeneous local horizons, minibatch sizes, gradient noise, and participation. We introduce HEW-Local SGD, a corrected local-SGD method that chooses nodewise server weights by minimizing an explicit one-round upper bound on the next objective value. This yields an exact local-control formulation with a threshold simplex update, separable amplitude updates, and a one-step guarantee under arbitrary predictable participation. We also introduce two post-local variants: a corrected heterogeneous method and a simpler homogeneous specialization. We establish one-step guarantees and global benchmark-style convergence results. In the regimes where comparison is appropriate, the theory matches the qualitative communication-efficient picture of recent LocalSGD/SCAFFOLD analyses, while also giving explicit guarantees for unequal local horizons.

</details>


### [182] [Scheduling and Routing in the Flexible Job Shop with Heterogeneous Transbots and Zoning: A Constraint Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24483)
*Arnovi Moinuddin,El Mehdi Er Raqabi,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对配备异构移动机器人（transbots）的柔性作业车间问题，提出两种集成生产调度与机器人路径规划的约束规划模型（基于弧的模型和操作嵌入模型），并设计了一种book-and-release策略来协调机器人移动，在分区约束、机器人异构性和区域间交接的复杂环境下实现了高质量求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造系统中移动运输机器人（transbots）的使用日益广泛，但分区约束、机器人异构性以及区域间交接点协调等因素使得生产调度与物料运输的联合优化面临巨大的计算复杂性，亟需有效的集成建模与求解方法。

Method: 提出了两种约束规划（CP）公式：弧基公式显式建模机器间转移，操作嵌入公式将运输决策嵌入操作调度结构中实现更紧的同步；同时设计了book-and-release策略协调机器人移动，无需强制执行刚性路径模式。

Result: 在基于案例研究改编的基准实例上，所提出的公式生成了高质量解，计算性能表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文为带有异构移动机器人和分区约束的柔性作业车间调度问题提供了有效的集成求解方案，并详细描述了实例生成方法以支持该新兴领域的后续研究。

Abstract: Coordinating production and material transfers is increasingly important in modern manufacturing systems equipped with mobile transfer robots, known as transbots. This study considers a flexible job shop environment in which heterogeneous transbots transport parts between machines. The shop floor is partitioned into zones, with each transbot assigned to a specific zone, and inter-zone movements are facilitated through designated handoff points. These zoning constraints, transbot heterogeneity, and inter-zone handoffs give rise to a challenging variant of the flexible job shop problem with embedded transbot-routing features, resulting in substantial computational complexity. Motivated by a real manufacturing setting, two constraint programming formulations that integrate production scheduling with transbot routing are proposed: an arc-based formulation that explicitly models machine-to-machine transfers, and an operation-embedded formulation that embeds transfer decisions directly within the operation scheduling structure, leading to tighter synchronization between production and transportation decisions. Both formulations capture machine flexibility, zoning restrictions, handoff coordination, and collision-free path planning. To efficiently solve the resulting problem, a book-and-release strategy is proposed. It coordinates transbot movements without enforcing rigid routing patterns. Computational experiments on case study-adapted benchmark-based demonstrate that the proposed formulations generate high-quality solutions with strong computational performance. The generation of instances is described in detail to support future work in this emerging area.

</details>


### [183] [PRP, HS and LS Conjugate Gradient Methods for Interval-Valued Multiobjective Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24605)
*Tapas Mondal,Debdulal Ghosh,Zai-Yun Peng,Yong Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无约束区间值多目标优化问题，提出了基于Polak-Ribiere-Polyak、Hestenes-Stiefel和Liu-Storey三种非线性共轭梯度法变体的高效算法，用于计算Pareto临界点。算法采用Wolfe线搜索策略确定步长，在适当假设下证明了全局收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无约束区间值多目标优化问题中计算Pareto临界点的需求，现有方法效率不足，需要开发更高效的算法。

Method: 基于PRP、HS和LS三种非线性共轭梯度法变体，结合Wolfe线搜索策略，构建了计算Pareto临界点的迭代算法。

Result: 在适当假设下建立了三种变体的全局收敛性理论，数值实验表明算法在基准测试问题上表现有效，并通过性能轮廓图分析进行了综合评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的三种非线性共轭梯度法变体能够高效求解区间值多目标优化问题，具有良好的收敛性和实用性。

Abstract: In this article, we develop an efficient algorithm based on three special variants of the nonlinear conjugate gradient method, namely, the Polak--Ribiere--Polyak, Hestenes--Stiefel, and Liu--Story schemes for computing Pareto critical points in unconstrained interval-valued multiobjective optimization problems. The proposed algorithm incorporates a Wolfe line search strategy to determine a suitable range of step size that satisfies the standard Wolfe conditions. For each of the proposed variants of the nonlinear conjugate gradient method, we establish rigorous global convergence results under appropriate assumptions. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods, we conduct numerical experiments on a set of benchmark test problems and present a comprehensive performance profile analysis.

</details>


### [184] [Dual Control of Linear Systems from Bilinear Observations with Belief Space Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24663)
*Daniel Cao,Beixi Du,Andrew Lowitt,Sunmook Choi,Sarah Dean,Yahya Sattar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有双线性观测的有限时域二次型控制系统，提出信念空间模型预测控制方法，通过直接规划状态估计和误差协方差，在数值实验中优于分离原则控制器及其MPC变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双线性观测系统中，控制输入不仅影响状态动态，还影响状态的部分观测，导致分离原则失效。控制输入会影响未来状态估计的质量，因此需要新的控制方法。

Method: 提出信念空间模型预测控制（B-MPC）方法，直接对估计状态及其误差协方差进行规划。使用依赖于输入的卡尔曼滤波定义信念演化的确定性替代模型。

Result: 在两组合成数值实验中，B-MPC在有利条件下优于分离原则控制器及其MPC变体，表现出更低的估计协方差和更具备不确定性意识的动作选择。

Conclusion: 信念空间MPC能够有效处理双线性观测系统中分离原则失效的问题，通过主动感知控制提升系统性能。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon quadratic control of linear systems with bilinear observations, in which the control input affects not only the state dynamics but also the partial observations of the state. In this setting, the separation principle can fail because control inputs influence the future quality of state estimates. State estimation requires an input-dependent Kalman filter whose gain and error covariance evolve as functions of the control inputs. To address this challenge, we propose a belief-space model predictive control ($\texttt{B-MPC}$) method that plans directly over both the estimated state and its error covariance.
